  80,\n386\t\t\theight:         24,\n387\t\t\tconnected:      false,\n388\t\t}\n389\t}\n390\t\n391\t// Init initializes the model.\n392\tfunc (m model) Init() tea.Cmd {\n393\t\treturn tea.Batch(\n394\t\t\tm.connectCmd(),\n395\t\t\ttickCmd(),\n396\t\t)\n397\t}\n398\t\n399\t// Update handles messages and updates the model.\n400\tfunc (m model) Update(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {\n401\t\tswitch msg := msg.(type) {\n402\t\tcase tea.KeyMsg:\n403\t\t\tswitch msg.String() {\n404\t\t\tcase \"q\", \"ctrl+c\":\n405\t\t\t\tm.quitting = true\n406\t\t\t\treturn m, tea.Quit\n407\t\t\tcase \"r\":\n408\t\t\t\t// Force reconnect\n409\t\t\t\tif m.conn != nil {\n410\t\t\t\t\tm.conn.Close()\n411\t\t\t\t\tm.conn = nil\n412\t\t\t\t}\n413\t\t\t\tm.connected = false\n414\t\t\t\treturn m, m.connectCmd()\n415\t\t\t}\n416\t\tcase connectedMsg:\n417\t\t\tm.connected = true\n418\t\t\tm.lastError = nil\n419\t\t\tm.reconnectDelay = time.Second\n420\t\t\tif m.conn != nil {\n421\t\t\t\tm.conn.Close()\n422\t\t\t}\n423\t\t\tm.conn = msg.Conn\n424\t\t\treturn m, waitForNextEvent(msg.Conn)\n425\t\tcase disconnectedMsg:\n426\t\t\tm.connected = false\n427\t\t\tm.lastError = msg.err\n428\t\t\tif m.conn != nil {\n429\t\t\t\tm.conn.Close()\n430\t\t\t\tm.conn = nil\n431\t\t\t}\n432\t\t\treturn m, tea.Tick(m.reconnectDelay, func(t time.Time) tea.Msg {\n433\t\t\t\treturn connectedMsg{}\n434\t\t\t})\n435\t\tcase eventWithConnMsg:\n436\t\t\t// Store the connection and handle the event\n437\t\t\tif m.conn != nil {\n438\t\t\t\tm.conn.Close()\n439\t\t\t}\n440\t\t\tm.conn = msg.Conn\n441\t\t\tm.connected = true\n442\t\t\tm.lastError = nil\n443\t\t\tm.handleEvent(msg.Event)\n444\t\t\treturn m, waitForNextEvent(msg.Conn)\n445\t\tcase eventReceivedMsg:\n446\t\t\tm.handleEvent(Event(msg))\n447\t\t\treturn m, m.readEventsCmd()\n448\t\tcase tickMsg:\n449\t\t\treturn m, tickCmd()\n450\t\tcase tea.WindowSizeMsg:\n451\t\t\tm.width = msg.Width\n452\t\t\tm.height = msg.Height\n453\t\t\treturn m, nil\n454\t\t}\n455\t\treturn m, nil\n456\t}\n457\t\n458\t// getFloat64 extracts a float64 value from an event field.\n459\tfunc getFloat64(event Event, key string) float64 {\n460\t\tif v, ok := event[key]; ok {\n461\t\t\tswitch val := v.(type) {\n462\t\t\tcase float64:\n463\t\t\t\treturn val\n464\t\t\tcase int:\n465\t\t\t\treturn float64(val)\n466\t\t\tcase int64:\n467\t\t\t\treturn float64(val)\n468\t\t\t}\n469\t\t}\n470\t\treturn 0\n471\t}\n472\t\n473\t// getInt64 extracts an int64 value from an event field.\n474\tfunc getInt64(event Event, key string) int64 {\n475\t\treturn int64(getFloat64(event, key))\n476\t}\n477\t\n478\t// getString extracts a string value from an event field.\n479\tfunc getString(event Event, key string) string {\n480\t\tif v, ok := event[key]; ok {\n481\t\t\tif s, ok := v.(string); ok {\n482\t\t\t\treturn s\n483\t\t\t}\n484\t\t}\n485\t\treturn \"\"\n486\t}\n487\t\n488\t// getBool extracts a bool value from an event field.\n489\tfunc getBool(event Event, key string) bool {\n490\t\tif v, ok := event[key]; ok {\n491\t\t\tif b, ok := v.(bool); ok {\n492\t\t\t\treturn b\n493\t\t\t}\n494\t\t}\n495\t\treturn false\n496\t}\n497\t\n498\t// handleEvent processes a single event from the stream.\n499\tfunc (m *model) handleEvent(event Event) {\n500\t\teventType := getString(event, \"type\")\n501\t\n502\t\tswitch eventType {\n503\t\t// Phase 6 Event Types\n504\t\tcase \"message_in\":\n505\t\t\ttopic := getString(event, \"topic\")\n506\t\t\tuser := getString(event, \"user\")\n507\t\t\tpreview := getString(event, \"preview\")\n508\t\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"MSG IN %s %s: %s\", topic, user, preview))\n509\t\t\tm.state.IncrementActiveMessageCount()\n510\t\n511\t\tcase \"message_out\":\n512\t\t\tchatID := getInt64(event, \"chat_id\")\n513\t\t\tthreadID := getInt64(event, \"thread_id\")\n514\t\t\ttopic := getString(event, \"topic\")\n515\t\t\tstatus := getString(event, \"status\")\n516\t\t\ttokens := int(getFloat64(event, \"tokens\"))\n517\t\t\tcostUSD := getFloat64(event, \"cost_usd\")\n518\t\n519\t\t\tif status == \"streaming\" {\n520\t\t\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"MSG OUT %s streaming %d tokens\", topic, tokens))\n521\t\t\t} else if status == \"complete\" {\n522\t\t\t\tm.state.AddCostEntry(chatID, threadID, costUSD, \"\")\n523\t\t\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"MSG OUT %s done %d tokens $%.4f\", topic, tokens, costUSD))\n524\t\t\t\tm.state.DecrementActiveMessageCount()\n525\t\t\t}\n526\t\n527\t\tcase \"command\":\n528\t\t\tcommand := getString(event, \"command\")\n529\t\t\targs := getString(event, \"args\")\n530\t\t\ttopic := getString(event, \"topic\")\n531\t\t\tuser := getString(event, \"user\")\n532\t\t\tresult := getString(event, \"result\")\n533\t\t\tm.state.AddCommandEntry(command+\" \"+args, 0, result == \"ok\")\n534\t\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"CMD %s %s %s %s → %s\", topic, user, command, args, result))\n535\t\n536\t\tcase \"session_update\":\n537\t\t\tchatID := getInt64(event, \"chat_id\")\n538\t\t\tthreadID := getInt64(event, \"thread_id\")\n539\t\t\ttopic := getString(event, \"topic\")\n540\t\t\tstatus := getString(event, \"status\")\n541\t\t\tmodel := getString(event, \"model\")\n542\t\t\tm.state.UpdateSession(chatID, threadID, \"\", topic, model, status, \"live\", \"\", 0, 0)\n543\t\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"SESSION %s %s: %s (%s)\", topic, status, model, status))\n544\t\n545\t\tcase \"health\":\n546\t\t\tproxyOK := getBool(event, \"proxy_ok\")\n547\t\t\tdbOK := getBool(event, \"db_ok\")\n548\t\t\tproxyLatencyMs := getInt64(event, \"proxy_latency_ms\")\n549\t\t\tdbLatencyMs := getInt64(event, \"db_latency_ms\")\n550\t\t\ttgPolling := getBool(event, \"tg_polling\")\n551\t\t\tbridgeUptimeSeconds := getInt64(event, \"bridge_uptime_seconds\")\n552\t\n553\t\t\t// Get last update ID (may be null)\n554\t\t\tvar lastUpdateIDPtr *int64\n555\t\t\tif v, ok := event[\"tg_last_update_id\"]; ok && v != nil {\n556\t\t\t\tif Healthy: proxyOK, Message: fmt.Sprintf(\"%dms\", proxyLatencyMs)},\n564\t\t\t\t{Name: \"database\", Healthy: dbOK, Message: fmt.Sprintf(\"%dms\", dbLatencyMs)},\n565\t\t\t}\n566\t\t\tm.state.UpdateHealthFull(proxyOK && dbOK, checks, tgPolling, lastUpdateIDPtr, bridgeUptimeSeconds)\n567\t\n568\t\t// Legacy Event Types (for backward compatibility)\n569\t\tcase \"session_created\", \"session_updated\":\n570\t\t\tchatID := getInt64(event, \"chat_id\")\n571\t\t\tthreadID := getInt64(event, \"thread_id\")\n572\t\t\tsessionID := getString(event, \"session_id\")\n573\t\t\ttopic := getString(event, \"topic\")\n574\t\t\tmodel := getString(event, \"model\")\n575\t\t\tstatus := getString(event, \"status\")\n576\t\t\tnotifMode := getString(event, \"notification_mode\")\n577\t\t\tif notifMode == \"\" {\n578\t\t\t\tnotifMode = \"live\"\n579\t\t\t}\n580\t\t\tcwd := getString(event, \"cwd\")\n581\t\t\tmsgCount := msgCount, cost)\n584\t\n585\t\tcase \"session_closed\":\n586\t\t\tchatID := getInt64(event, \"chat_id\")\n587\t\t\tthreadID := getInt64(event, \"thread_id\")\n588\t\t\tm.state.RemoveSession(chatID, \"message_id\")\n594\t\t\tcontentType := getString(event, \"content_type\")\n595\t\t\tuserID := getInt64(event, \"user_id\")\n596\t\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"MSG IN ch=%d th=%d msg=%d \"model\")\n620\t\t\tm.state.AddCostEntry(chatID, threadID, costUSD, model)\n621\t\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"COST ch=%d th=%d $%.4f %s\", chatID, threadID, costUSD, {\n627\t\t\t\tfor _, checkItem := range c {\n628\t\t\t\t\tif checkMap, ok := checkItem.(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n629\t\t\t\t\t\tcheck := HealthCheck{}\n630\t\t\t\t\t\tif name, ok := \"system_info\":\n651\t\t\tmessage := getString(event, \"message\")\n652\t\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"INFO %s\", message))\n653\t\t}\n654\t}\n655\t\n656\t// View renders the UI.\n657\tfunc (m model) View() string {\n658\t\tif m.quitting {\n659\t\t\treturn \"Goodbye!\\n\"\n660\t\t}\n661\t\n662\t\t// Define styles\n663\t\ttitleStyle := := cmdEntries[i]\n833\t\t\tcmdStyle := successStyle\n834\t\t\tif !cmd.Success {\n835\t\t\t\tcmdStyle = errorStyle\n836\t\t\t}\n837\t\t\tcmdPanel += dimStyle.Render(cmd.Time+\" \") +\n838\t\t\t\tcmdStyle.Render(truncateString(cmd.Command, 30)) +\n839\t\t\t\tdimStyle.Render(fmt.Sprintf(\" uid=%d\", cmd.UserID)) + \"\\n\"\n840\t\t}\n841\t\n842\t\t// Cost Tracker The bridge must support long-running invocations, cancellation, and non-blocking operation before the orchestrator/worker model can work reliably. Non-blocking worker (background goroutine per invocation), timeout raised to 1800s, `timeout_sec=0` for no deadline, subprocess context decoupled from Telegram send context.\n863\t\n864\t### 7.1 — In-flight cancellation\n865\t\n866\t**Problem:** A stuck or long-running invocation holds the topic worker goroutine with no escape.\n867\t\n868\t**Solution:** Track the active `*exec.Cmd` in `topicWorker`. A `/cancel` command (general topic or in-topic) sends `SIGTERM` to the running process.\n869\t\n870\t```go\n871\ttype topicWorker struct {\n872\t    ch        chan sessionMsg\n873\t    cancel    context.CancelFunc\n874\t    activeCmd *exec.Cmd   // guarded by activeMu\n875\t    activeMu  sync.Mutex\n876\t}\n877\t```\n878\t\n879\t- Set `activeCmd` when `cmd.Start()` succeeds; clear it after `cmd.Wait()` returns.\n880\t- `/cancel [thread_id]` command: acquire `activeMu`, call `activeCmd.Process.Signal(os.Interrupt)`.\n881\t- Edit the \"Thinking…\" placeholder to \"⚠️ Cancelled\" on cancellation.\n882\t- Topic color → red on cancel.\n883\t\n884\t### 7.2 — Extended timeouts + per-topic timeout override\n885\t\n886\t**Problem:** `defaultSessionTimeout = 300` (5 min) is too low for agentic tasks. Per-topic override is missing.\n887\t\n888\t**Changes:**\n889\t- Raise `defaultSessionTimeout` from `300` → `1800` (30 min).\n890\t- Add `timeout_sec` column to `sessions` table (mirrors `groups.timeout_sec`). Per-topic override takes precedence over group default.\n891\t- Add `/timeout [N]` as a topic-level command (currently only group-level via `/config`).\n892\t- `N=0` disables the timeout entirely (no `context.WithTimeout`).\n893\t- Add `/timeout` to `/help` output.\n894\t\n895\t**Timeout resolution order** (first non-zero wins):\n896\t1. `sessions.timeout_sec` — topic-level override\n897\t2. `groups.timeout_sec` — group default\n898\t3. `defaultSessionTimeout` — hardcoded fallback processed by the bridge\n- Shows command, topic, user, result (OK/error)\n- Ring buffer of last ~50 commands\n\n#### Cost Tracker\n- Aggregated cost from session `total_cost_usd` fields\n- Today's total, current hour, and active session count\n- Per-session breakdown available by selecting a session in the Active Sessions panel\n\n### Event Stream\n\nThe bridge publishes events to a local Unix socket at `/tmp/telegram-bridge-events.sock` (or configurable path). The event protocol is NDJSON with event types:\n\n```json\n{\"type\":\"message_in\",\"timestamp\":\"...\",\"chat_id\":...,\"thread_id\":...,\"topic\":\"#trading-bot\",\"user\":\"@jed\",\"preview\":\"add stop-loss logic\"}\n{\"type\":\"message_out\",\"timestamp\":\"...\",\"chat_id\":...,\"thread_id\":...,\"topic\":\"#trading-bot\",\"status\":\"streaming\",\"tokens\":340,\"elapsed_ms\":1200}\n{\"type\":\"message_out\",\"timestamp\":\"...\",\"chat_id\":...,\"thread_id\":...,\"topic\":\"#trading-bot\",\"status\":\"complete\",\"tokens\":1200,\"cost_usd\":0.032,\"elapsed_ms\":4800}\n{\"type\":\"command\",\"timestamp\":\"...\",\"command\":\"/model\",\"args\":\"opus\",\"topic\":\"#new-feature\",\"user\":\"@jed\",\"result\":\"ok\"}\n{\"type\":\"session_update\",\"timestamp\":\"...\",\"chat_id\":...,\"thread_id\":...,\"topic\":\"#new-feature\",\"status\":\"active\",\"model\":\"claude-opus-4-6\"}\n{\"type\":\"health\",\"timestamp\":\"...\",\"proxy_ok\":true,\"proxy_latency_ms\":210,\"db_ok\":true,\"db_latency_ms\":6}\n```\n\nThe bridge emits events regardless of whether the TUI is connected. If no listener is present, writes are silently dropped (non-blocking).\n\n### Running\n\n```bash\n# In a tmux session\ntmux new -s dashboard\n./bin/dashboard\n\n# Or with a custom socket path\n./bin/dashboard --socket /tmp/telegram-bridge-events.sock\n```\n\nThe dashboard is optional — the bridge functions identically without it. It's a pure observer for operational visibility.\n\n**Deliverable:** Real-time terminal dashboard for monitoring bridge activity, session state, and costs.\n\n## Phase 7: Foundational Infrastructure for Dispatcher\n\n### Purpose\n\nPrerequisite pieces that the Phase 9 dispatcher architecture depends on. The bridge must support long-running invocations, cancellation, and non-blocking operation before the orchestrator/worker model can work reliably. Phases 7 and 9 are designed together — Phase 7 is the infrastructure layer, Phase 9 is the dispatch layer built on top of it.\n\n**Status of 7.1–7.2:** Implemented. Non-blocking worker (background goroutine per invocation), timeout raised to 1800s, `timeout_sec=0` for no deadline, subprocess context decoupled from Telegram send context.\n\n### 7.1 — In-flight cancellation\n\n**Problem:** A stuck or long-running invocation holds the topic worker goroutine with no escape.\n\n**Solution:** Track the active `*exec.Cmd` in `topicWorker`. A `/cancel` command (general topic or in-topic) sends `SIGTERM` to the running process.\n\n```go\ntype topicWorker struct {\n    ch        chan sessionMsg\n    cancel    context.CancelFunc\n    activeCmd *exec.Cmd   // guarded by activeMu\n    activeMu  sync.Mutex\n}\n```\n\n- Set `activeCmd` when `cmd.Start()` succeeds; clear it after `cmd.Wait()` returns.\n- `/cancel [thread_id]` command: acquire `activeMu`, call `activeCmd.Process.Signal(os.Interrupt)`.\n- Edit the \"Thinking…\" placeholder to \"⚠️ Cancelled\" on cancellation.\n- Topic color → red on cancel.\n\n### 7.2 — Extended timeouts + per-topic timeout override\n\n**Problem:** `defaultSessionTimeout = 300` (5 min) is too low for agentic tasks. Per-topic override is missing.\n\n**Changes:**\n- Raise `defaultSessionTimeout` from `300` → `1800` (30 min).\n- Add `timeout_sec` column to `sessions` table (mirrors `groups.timeout_sec`). Per-topic override takes precedence over group default.\n- Add `/timeout [N]` as a topic-level command (currently only group-level via `/config`).\n- `N=0` disables the timeout entirely (no `context.WithTimeout`).\n- Add `/timeout` to `/help` output.\n\n**Timeout resolution order** (first non-zero wins):\n1. `sessions.timeout_sec` — topic-level override\n2. `groups.timeout_sec` — group default\n3. `defaultSessionTimeout` — hardcoded fallback (1800s)\n","numLines":100,"startLine":800,"totalLines":1453}}}
